**鍏抽敭璋冪敤閾捐矾锛?*

1. **椋庨櫓璇勪及鍏ㄩ摼璺?*锛歚risk_service.assess_risk()` 鈫?骞惰璋冪敤 `coordination_service` + `propagation_service` + `account_service` 鈫?`evidence_builder` 鈫?`phase_detector` 鈫?`ds_fusion` 鈫?`disarm_scorer` 鈫?`report_builder` 鈫?鎸佷箙鍖?MySQL
2. **鏁版嵁閲囬泦閾捐矾**锛歚crawl_service.create_crawl_job()` 鈫?Celery `execute_crawl_job` 鈫?`CrawlerFactory` 鈫?鍐欏叆 MongoDB
3. **瓒嬪娍棰勬祴閾捐矾**锛歚propagation_service.predict_propagation_trend()` 鈫?`ts_features` 鈫?`llm_context` 鈫?`regime_model` 鈫?`trend_predictor`

## 5.15 鍏抽敭鎶€鏈?I/O 鎺ュ彛瀹氫箟锛圓RIS 杈圭晫锛孈version: v1锛?
鏈妭瀹氫箟涓変釜 ARIS锛圓lgorithm-Runtime Interface Specification锛夊叧閿妧鏈帴鍙ｏ紝浣滀负绠楁硶鏍稿績妯″潡涓庝笟鍔℃湇鍔″眰鐨勫绾﹁竟鐣屻€傛墍鏈夋帴鍙ｄ娇鐢?Python `dataclass` 鏍煎紡瀹氫箟锛岀‘淇濈被鍨嬪畨鍏ㄥ拰鍙簭鍒楀寲銆?
> 浠ｇ爜寮曠敤锛歚app/core/coordination_baseline/detector.py`銆乣app/core/propagation/trend_predictor.py`銆乣app/core/review/disarm_scorer.py`

### 5.15.1 鍗忓悓妫€娴嬫帴鍙ｏ紙@version: v1锛?
```python
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime

@dataclass
class CoordinationDetectionInput:
    """鍗忓悓妫€娴嬭緭鍏ャ€侤version: v1

    鐢?coordination_service 浠?MongoDB 鍘熷鏁版嵁鏋勫缓锛?    浼犲叆 core/coordination 绠楁硶妯″潡銆?    """
    # 鍏变韩琛屼负璁板綍琛紙蹇呴』鍖呭惈 object_id, account_id, content_id, timestamp_share锛?    records: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)

    # 妫€娴嬪弬鏁?    time_window: int = 60               # 鍗忓悓鏃堕棿绐楀彛锛堢锛夛紝1-3600
    min_participation: int = 2           # 鏈€浣庡弬涓庢鏁?    edge_weight_percentile: float = 0.5  # 杈规潈鐧惧垎浣嶉槇鍊硷紝0-1
    remove_loops: bool = True            # 鏄惁绉婚櫎鑷幆

    # 鍙€変笂涓嬫枃
    platform: str | None = None          # 闄愬畾骞冲彴


@dataclass
class CoordinationDetectionOutput:
    """鍗忓悓妫€娴嬭緭鍑恒€侤version: v1

    鐢?core/coordination 绠楁硶妯″潡浜у嚭锛?    渚?risk_service 鐨勮瘉鎹瀯寤哄櫒娑堣垂銆?    """
    # 缃戠粶缁撴瀯
    network: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    # 缁撴瀯: {nodes: [{id, ...}], edges: [{source, target, weight, avg_time_delta, edge_symmetry_score}],
    #        node_count, edge_count, component_count, components: [{size, members}]}

    # 缁熻淇℃伅
    account_stats: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)  # 姣忚处鎴风粺璁?    group_stats: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)     # 姣忕粍浠剁粺璁?
    # 鎽樿
    total_posts: int = 0
    total_pairs: int = 0
    coordinated_accounts: int = 0
    coordinated_edges: int = 0
    component_count: int = 0

    # 閿欒淇℃伅锛堥潪绌烘椂琛ㄧず妫€娴嬪け璐ワ級
    error: str | None = None
```

### 5.15.2 瓒嬪娍棰勬祴鎺ュ彛锛園version: v1锛?
```python
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

@dataclass
class TrendPredictionInput:
    """瓒嬪娍棰勬祴杈撳叆銆侤version: v1

    鐢?propagation_service 浠?MongoDB 鍘熷鏁版嵁鏋勫缓锛?    浼犲叆 core/propagation/trend_predictor 妯″潡銆?    """
    # 甯栧瓙鍒楄〃锛堝繀椤诲寘鍚?timestamp 瀛楁锛?    posts: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)

    # 璇勮鍒楄〃锛堝彲閫夛紝棰勭暀鎺ュ彛锛?    comments: list[dict] | None = None

    # Coordination Discover 鍗忓悓妫€娴嬩俊鍙凤紙鍙€夛紝璺ㄦā鍧楄緭鍏ワ級
    coordination_signals: dict[str, Any] | None = None

    # 鎺у埗鍙傛暟
    mock_llm: bool = False               # 鏄惁璺宠繃 LLM API 璋冪敤
    forecast_horizons: list[float] = field(default_factory=lambda: [1.0, 6.0, 24.0])  # 棰勬祴鏃堕棿璺ㄥ害锛堝皬鏃讹級

    # 鍙€変笂涓嬫枃
    platform: str | None = None


@dataclass
class TrendPredictionOutput:
    """瓒嬪娍棰勬祴杈撳嚭銆侤version: v1

    鐢?core/propagation/trend_predictor 浜у嚭锛?    渚涘墠绔湅鏉垮拰椋庨櫓璇勪及娑堣垂銆?    """
    # 浼犳挱閲忛娴?    volume_forecast: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
    # 缁撴瀯: {"1h": 120, "6h": 450, "24h": 800}

    # 缃俊鍖洪棿
    confidence_interval: dict[str, list[int]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    # 缁撴瀯: {"1h": [80, 160], "6h": [300, 600], "24h": [500, 1100]}

    # 鏂瑰悜鍒ゆ柇
    direction: str = "stable"  # rising | stable | declining

    # 閫熷害淇℃伅
    speed: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    # 缁撴瀯: {"acceleration": 2.5, "phase": "amplification"}

    # 浣撳埗鍚庨獙姒傜巼
    regime_posterior: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    # 缁撴瀯: {"seeding": 0.1, "amplification": 0.6, "peak": 0.2, "decay": 0.1}

    # 妫€娴嬪埌鐨勫鐢熶簨浠?    detected_events: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    # 姣忛」: {"type": "kol_amplification", "evidence": "...", "confidence": 0.8}

    # 棰勬祴缃俊搴︼紙0-1锛?    confidence: float = 0.0

    # 涓枃瓒嬪娍瑙ｉ噴
    explanation: str = ""

    # LLM 鏄惁鍙敤
    llm_available: bool = False

    # 鏃跺簭鐗瑰緛鎽樿
    ts_features: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    # 缁撴瀯: {current_volume, velocity, acceleration, burst_zscore, hours_since_start}
```

### 5.15.3 鏀诲嚮鍒嗘瀽鎺ュ彛锛園version: v1锛?
```python
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

@dataclass
class AttackAnalysisInput:
    """DISARM 鏀诲嚮璺緞鍒嗘瀽杈撳叆銆侤version: v1

    鐢?risk_service 鍦ㄩ闄╄瘎浼版祦姘寸嚎涓瀯寤猴紝
    浼犲叆 core/review/disarm_scorer 妯″潡銆?    """
    # 涓婃父璇佹嵁鍖咃紙鐢?evidence_builder 鏋勫缓锛?    evidence_pack: "EvidencePack" = None  # type: ignore

    # 闃舵妫€娴嬬粨鏋滐紙鐢?phase_detector 浜у嚭锛?    phase_result: "PhaseResult" = None    # type: ignore

    # D-S 铻嶅悎缁撴灉锛堢敱 ds_fusion 浜у嚭锛?    fusion_result: "FusionResult" = None  # type: ignore

    # 鍙€夐厤缃鐩?    config_overrides: dict = field(default_factory=dict)


@dataclass
class ObservedTechniqueItem:
    """瑙傛祴鍒扮殑 DISARM 鎶€鏈€?""
    technique_id: str = ""    # 濡?"T0105"
    tactic: str = ""          # 濡?"TA10"
    name: str = ""            # 濡?"Coordinate Activity"
    belief: float = 0.0       # 淇″康鍊?0-1
    evidence: str = ""        # 璇佹嵁鎻忚堪


@dataclass
class PredictedTechniqueItem:
    """棰勬祴鐨勪笅涓€姝ユ妧鏈€?""
    technique_id: str = ""
    name: str = ""
    probability: float = 0.0  # 杞崲姒傜巼 0-1


@dataclass
class CountermeasureItem:
    """鍙嶅埗寤鸿銆?""

    priority: str = ""         # critical/high/medium/low
    probability: float = 0.0   # 鍏宠仈姒傜巼


@dataclass
class AttackPathScoreItem:
    """鏀诲嚮璺緞璇勫垎銆?""

    breadth: int = 0           # 璺緞骞垮害锛堣娴嬪埌鐨勬妧鏈暟锛?    completeness: float = 0.0  # 瀹屾暣搴︼紙瑕嗙洊鐨勬垬鏈瘮渚嬶級
    score: float = 0.0         # 缁煎悎璇勫垎 0-100


@dataclass
class AttackAnalysisOutput:
    """DISARM 鏀诲嚮璺緞鍒嗘瀽杈撳嚭銆侤version: v1

    鐢?core/review/disarm_scorer 浜у嚭锛?    渚?report_builder 缁勮鏈€缁堟姤鍛娿€?    """
    # 瑙傛祴鍒扮殑 DISARM 鎶€鏈垪琛?    observed_techniques: list[ObservedTechniqueItem] = field(default_factory=list)

    # 鏀诲嚮璺緞璇勫垎
    path_score: AttackPathScoreItem = field(default_factory=AttackPathScoreItem)

    # 棰勬祴鐨勪笅涓€姝ユ妧鏈?    predicted_next: list[PredictedTechniqueItem] = field(default_factory=list)

    # 鍙嶅埗寤鸿锛堟寜浼樺厛绾ф帓搴忥級
    countermeasures: list[CountermeasureItem] = field(default_factory=list)
```

## 闄勫綍 C锛氶闄╃瓑绾т笌闃舵瀹氫箟

> 浠ｇ爜寮曠敤锛歚app/core/review/phase_detector.py`銆乣app/core/review/report_builder.py`

### C.1 椋庨櫓绛夌骇瀹氫箟

椋庨櫓绛夌骇鐢辩患鍚堥闄╄瘎鍒嗭紙`overall_risk_score`锛?-100锛夋槧灏勫緱鍑恒€傝瘎鍒嗗叕寮忓熀浜?D-S 铻嶅悎鍚庣殑淇″康鍖洪棿锛歚score = (belief 脳 0.7 + plausibility 脳 0.3) 脳 100`銆?
| 椋庨櫓绛夌骇 | 璇勫垎鑼冨洿 | 棰滆壊鏍囪瘑 | 澶勭疆寤鸿 |
|----------|----------|----------|----------|
| `low` | 0 - 25 | 缁胯壊 | 甯歌鐩戞帶锛屾棤闇€鐗瑰埆鍏虫敞 |
| `medium` | 26 - 50 | 榛勮壊 | 鍔犲己鐩戞帶锛屽叧娉ㄨ秼鍔垮彉鍖?|
| `high` | 51 - 75 | 姗欒壊 | 閲嶇偣鍏虫敞锛屽畨鎺掑垎鏋愬笀璺熻繘 |
| `critical` | 76 - 100 | 绾㈣壊 | 绔嬪嵆鍚姩搴旀€ュ搷搴?|

> 浠ｇ爜寮曠敤锛歚app/core/review/report_builder.py` 鈥?`_risk_level()` 鍑芥暟锛岄槇鍊煎彲閫氳繃 `risk_config.yaml` 鐨?`scoring.levels` 閰嶇疆銆?
### C.2 鎴樺焦鐢熷懡鍛ㄦ湡闃舵瀹氫箟
